## Stage 88 — OMS, CRM, Inventory and WMS as separate modules; one page per OMS step; menus instead of tab strips; Pending gate and two bills; one Templates page; Inventory documents (opened 2026-10-18 from the user's eight screenshots)

User, 2026-10-18, over four messages: *"CRM is separate and OMS is separate. Under OMS each module will be separate. Pick/Pack/Ship - Invoice/Label/Manifest three pages. Accept (Pending Order) one page. Then return. See all config in one page. It should be diff page. In Setup and Settings, config all in one looks scary. Group them and show in another sub menu ... Propose 2-3 alternatives."* - *"Manufacturing and PIMs also show as sub menu like other."* - *"Report also. It will be grouped with Sales and show sub group of Sales related in another flyout. Home will show only txn, no master data shortcut. Can't close this (RF). Inventory is diff and WMS is diff. Inv is part of ERP. Inv will have dashboard, ledger of inv and stock movement (location to location), adjustment, MRP change. All will have separate history page like we built for POS bill, PO."* - *"Update everything in checklist. It should be built and recorded, so no one should miss. We will have status of building as well."* Plan: `docs/product/navigation-simplification-plan-2026-10-18.md` (decisions D1-D14, same item IDs).

**Decisions (user, 2026-10-18, asked):** D1 Setup and Settings as a two-level menu (A of three alternatives). D2 marketplace / website orders stop at Pending until accepted or rejected, and the answer is sent to the channel. D3 Pick · Pack · Ship pages. D4 the OMS bill (own series, OMS template) goes to the customer; the ERP bill is made from it at manifest handover. D5 the OMS bill's number is the GST invoice; the ERP bill links to it and is not reported twice. D6 one Templates page under Setup, module by module. D7 Reports menu grouped by area. D8 Home shows transactions only. D9 Inventory (ERP) and WMS are separate modules. **Approved as proposed (user, 2026-10-18: "All approved which are recommended for me"):** D12 manual orders skip Pending; D13 Stock Adjustment needs a reason per line, goes through approval rules, posts ledger + GL; D14 MRP Change is item-level with an effective date and a sticker reprint offer.

Found while planning: working APIs with no screen - the pick worklist (`GET /api/v1/oms/pick-queue`, 35.3.4), packages and the pack invoice (`/api/v1/oms/shipping-packages`, 35.4.2), gate passes (35.4.4), `label.pdf`, and the channel status push (`PushChannelOrderStatus`). The Setup menu lists ~120 lists in one scroll (Stock 39). RF Lot & Serial's task menu has no exit. No Stock Adjustment or MRP Change document exists.

## Stage 89 — Item master: org defaults, one pick fills the item, every field asked once, attributes from masters, where each field is asked (Item / PO / GRN), attributes mapped to the type (opened 2026-10-18 from the user's Edit Item screenshots)

User, 2026-10-18, on the deployed **Edit Item** (antique necklace; Product Family *Anitque*; More details: Sub Category, Type, Device Master Record, Device Type / Category, Polish / Plating, Metal Type, Purity, Gross / Net Weight, Stone Type / Weight, Making Charge Type / Value, Size): *"What ever is there in item master, It will be some deafult config for org. if chang required in item user will change it. While creating item it self it should ask all this. User will never revisit this. & It need to smart filled/ Auto filled with a single selction. Tax treatment will deafut taxable. Ask family mandatory in top it self. Code auto generate. Tracabity, GRN batch one company level define and Item will inherit and offcourse user can chnage required. But ask in org level. All Shelf life default take std value. Weight and ask in GRN to fill. Catrgory/Type make mandtory. Type and Famiy will create Item name it can be a+b or b+a. Allow to change once in config. It will be reversible. Purity Ask in GRN, It will hidable in GRN as well. Give in config. What ever attribute to ask in PO and GRN, Need config. Polish, Color, all will be drop down. It should ask to add in Master if not present. There will be another logic. COlor, Polish, Size etc will have one master data individually. next Categorywise there will be another mapping for color,polish and size and other attribute. This applies to all industry. Make that flexible. While Creating PO, I order for Antique Necklace- Antique will be Catgory and necklace will be type. Now red color will be attached to necklace. Once I order necklace it will show necklace related color. So, This will be a config to turn on and off. If Off all attribute will show to al. Else I ahve to map attribute to type. By dafeaut it will be all. Bring this. This should be flexible and dynamic with new master created. If on, It should where to map. Smart As well."*

Then, on the form's **Barcode and label** and **Selling and accounting** sections (every dropdown showing *— Select Option —*): *"Same goes for barcode as well. Config at org level. Account as well. Make the default for normal prod. Make it easy for user, Ask What type of product. Very easy Std option you ask and as per that change it will define the accounting drop down. Make that field mandatory to Item creation as well. The drop down should be dead easy and changeable if user do mistake."*

**Found while planning (what exists).** Code is already numbered by the server (`Item/HQ/2026/000001`). **GRN batch number** is already org-level (`inventory.grn_batch_number`, 61.6) with Item Type and Item overrides (*Item type default*). **Traceability** (`tracking_mode`) and **shelf life** have no org default - blank on every new item. **Tax Treatment** is blank on a new item. Item **Category** is free text; there is **no Type** on Item. The **Category / Product Type / Item Name** masters exist (65.3: Item Name = *Category + Product Type*, holding HSN + GST) but only the PO uses them; the Item form does not. **Product Family** on the Item is the PO's *design* (65.4: design number, vendor, design attributes, item name) - not a category; the client's family *Anitque* is being used as one. The ERP's own **Item Attribute** master exists (86.3) and feeds the Item form's Variant Options and the PO; the jewellery industry profile separately adds **free-text** Color / Polish / Size / Stone Type and fixed-list Metal Type / Purity fields to Item (`public/profiles/jewelry.json`; the 81.6 overlap). The client's Item also carries **Device Master Record / Device Type** - fields a Medical profile added before the industry changed (82: a change adds, never deletes). The GRN line has no place for weight, purity or any attribute. Nothing maps attributes to a type. **Barcode** is already org-level (`inventory.barcode_generate_at`, `inventory.barcode_per_receipt_date`, 57.7) and the eight accounting dimensions already come from the **Item Type** profile (72.1: Item > Item Type > company default = ordinary merchandise); the 13 shipped profiles are the "type of product" (Product = normal product, Packaging, Gift, Free With Item, Service, Gift Card, Consumable - Periodic, Non-Stock Expense, Non-Asset, Accessory, Backflush, Fixed Asset, Virtual Kit). But Item Type is optional on the Item, and every inheriting dropdown shows a blank *— Select Option —*, so the form reads as nine unanswered questions when a normal product needs none. **Changeable after a mistake** meets 72.12: once an item has stock history, MASTER-0407 refuses a change to its stock tracking, valuation or accounting treatment (D5).

**Decisions (user, 2026-10-18, asked).** **D1** the pick at the top of New Item is the **Item name** (*Antique Necklace*): it fills Category, Type, HSN, GST and the type's attributes; a new one is made right there from Category + Type; Product Family stays the PO's design and is not asked - *"Option1 but I may change in config saying Item name can be Type+Category or Category+Type"*. **D2** switching the name order **renames existing item names too** (and back when reversed); bills already printed keep the name they printed. **D3** values asked at the GRN (weights, purity, …) are kept on the **GRN line and its batch** - stickers and the POS read them from the batch. **D5** **free until stock moves; after that an admin-approved correction** - first answered *free until stock moves*, then (same day): *"Admin change + correction ... this is also required. Tell the impact in detail and show preview of impact and Admin approve and it will change. Warn and Change after multiple Confirmation. Are you sure one. Multiple mean no 100 times. In the std way how many time it should ask. We should give all flexibility to user. We will not build again this. May case come admin identify later after GRN."* So: until the first stock movement the kind of item and its accounting change freely; after it, a change to stock tracking / valuation / accounting treatment goes through **89.8** (impact preview → reason + one typed confirmation → admin approval → applied with its correcting journal) instead of being refused. Two confirmations, the standard: the reviewed preview with a reason, then typing the item code. **D4 taken without asking** (user: *"make that flexible"*): attributes map to a **Type** (the user's necklace example), a **Category** may be mapped too, and the more specific mapping wins (Type over Category), as Backflush rules resolve (72.3). Also taken without asking (reversible, say if wrong): org defaults live in **Configuration → Inventory** beside the existing GRN batch setting; an item inherits a default by holding *Company default*, the pattern `grn_batch_number` already uses, so changing the org default moves every inheriting item; attribute-to-type mapping works at both levels (which attributes a type has, and optionally which values); with mapping on, a type with nothing mapped still shows everything, so turning it on never empties a form.

## Stage 90 — Version numbers follow each release (opened + built 2026-10-18 from the user's Control Panel screenshot)

User, 2026-10-18: *"How come version is not changing its 0.1.0. It should be linked to the release built a mechanism for that. Like: 840909b is released. Now I want that to be mapped to our logic of 0.1.0 how it rule is set. It should update accordingly and show accordingly. I can see in control panel the mapping but it should update."*

Why it was stuck: the number came from `internal/server/VERSION` (hand-edited, `0.1.0` since Stage 14.6; Stage 54 kept it there). The Releases table reads `public.deployments`, which only `promote.ps1` wrote. A client's Version is `public.tenants.app_version`, stamped once when the client was provisioned.

| Phase | What | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 90.1 | Numbering rule + stamping | Built, not verified live |
| 90.2 | Ledger row + client stamp at each release | Built, not verified live |
| 90.3 | `840909b` recorded as 0.1.0 | Not started (at the next deploy) |
| 90.4 | Guides and KB | Done |

- [ ] **90.1 The rule and the stamp.** Semantic versioning from the commits since the live build: any `feat:` → MINOR (0.1.0 → 0.2.0); only fixes / docs / chores → PATCH (0.2.0 → 0.2.1); MAJOR only by hand (`deploy.ps1 -Version 1.0.0`). `engines/release_versions.go` `NextReleaseVersion`; `cmd/releasever` reads `git log <live commit>..HEAD`. `deploy.ps1` reads the live version + commit from the box's `/api/v1/version`, gets the number, and stamps `-X internal/server.releaseVersion`. `currentAppVersion()` prefers the stamp, else the VERSION file, so the sidebar, `/api/v1/version` and the Control Panel all follow. Tests: `TestNextReleaseVersion`, `TestStampedReleaseVersionWins`; `cmd/releasever` gives 0.1.1 from 840909b (docs only) and 0.2.0 from 0f2e6e1 (a feat). *Status: Built, not verified live*
- [x] **90.2 Record the release.** `erp-server -record-release` (run by `remote_deploy.sh` once the health check passes; a failure warns, the deploy stands) writes `public.deployments` (environment, version, commit, by, note "from 0.1.0 (840909b)", `passed`) and sets every unpinned client's `app_version`. A re-run adds no second row. `-version/-commit/-at` record an earlier build without touching clients. Test: `TestRecordReleaseLedgerAndClientStamp`. *Status: Done - verified live 2026-10-19: the 0.2.0 deploy printed "Release 0.2.0 (455f4f9) recorded; 2 client(s) now show it". The 0.1.0 backfill row for 840909b is still to be run (refused in-session as a remote write)*
- [ ] **90.3 Map 840909b.** At the next deploy, record `840909b` as `0.1.0` (the number it ran under, deployed 2026-10-18 04:45:51 UTC) with `-record-release -version 0.1.0 -commit 840909b -at ...`; that deploy becomes 0.2.0 by the rule. [Waits for the user's go-ahead to push and deploy.] *Status: Not started*
- [x] **90.4 Guides.** ADMIN_GUIDE §C.2.1 (rule, stamp, override, backfill, VERSION file's new role) and §C.8 (Version column, Releases table); USER_GUIDE §11.1 (how the number goes up); new KB article `platform-control-panel.md`. *Status: Done*

## Stage 91 — A clean slate on app and minn, and every setting asked in the UI (opened 2026-10-18 from the user's message; parked until modules 3-5 are done)

User, 2026-10-18: *"Clean up what ever required you do for both app and minn. I can start as clean slate"* - then, asked what to keep: production **app + minn**; *"Do all cleanup except login pasword for both. But it should be std way from now, No code ask, Ultra userfrirendly. Ask to set up where ever required inUI. All the config in detail it should ask."*; document numbers **restart from 1**. Then: *"Yes commit 1-2 and start 3-4-5"* - so this waits until modules 3-5 of the build order are done.

**Why it needs care.** Both are live production clients; a wipe cannot be undone except from a backup. The repo already has `cmd/tenantctl` (operator-only, dry run by default, `-reason` / `-actor`, an evidence trail) and the setup advisor (`engines/setup_advisor.go`) - the reset and the guided setup build on those rather than a one-off SQL script.

| Phase | What | Status |
|---|---|---|
| A | Reset tool (operator, dry run, backup first) | Done (2026-10-19, "build all" step 3) - not run on app / minn |
| B | Guided first-run setup in the UI | Needs decision (scope) |
| C | Run on app and minn, verify | Blocked on A, B and the user's go |

- [x] **91.1 Reset a client to a clean slate - a standard operator action.** `tenantctl reset-data` (dry run by default; `-yes`, `-reason`, `-actor`; refuses without a backup newer than the run): removes every business record - transactions, stock, ledger, masters, company setup and settings - and keeps the logins: users with their password hashes and the roles they need to sign in, plus what the app needs to run (record definitions, permissions). Prints per-table counts before it changes anything; evidence in `tenant_lifecycle_events`. Never changes a password.
  **Built 2026-10-19 ("build all", step 3):** `tenantctl reset-data -tenant <id> [-reason -actor -yes]` → `engines/tenant_reset.go`. The plan lists every table (clear / keep / reset, rows, why) and the records removed and kept by type; `-yes` refuses unless the client is **suspended**, a backup of it from the last 24 hours is recorded (the renumbering rule, `recodeBackupStatus`), there is no legal hold, and every table is classified. One transaction; `data_reset` evidence row. Kept: users (password hashes untouched), MFA codes, roles and permissions, record definitions, chart of accounts, approval rules, loyalty tiers, stock location types, module entitlements / limits / flags, the shipped reference records (status rules, item types, TDS sections, stored-value profiles, currencies, tenders, HSN list, core attributes - rows created by `system`), and evidence (audit trail, privacy requests, e-invoice history, error log). Everything else goes, settings back to defaults and the chosen industry asked again. Done in place, not by rebuilding the schema, so the append-only triggers and per-schema functions stay. Tests: `TestTenantResetClassifiesEveryTable` (fails when a later table is not classified), `TestStage91TenantDataReset` (a provisioned client with data: refused while active and with a 3-day-old backup; then reset - only shipped records left, stock / counters / settings empty, a login's hash unchanged, permissions kept, formats shipped, evidenced once, the next PO number starts from 1). Dry run checked on the local `tenant_new`. ADMIN_GUIDE §C.6. *Status: Done*
- [x] **91.2 Numbering starts again from 1** after a reset (sequence counters cleared; series formats back to the shipped ones). **Built 2026-10-19 with 91.1:** `sequence_counters` emptied; `prefix_configs` replaced from the template client `tenant_default` column by column (the template client itself keeps its formats - it is what "shipped" means on that server). Tested (next number after a reset ends in 1). *Status: Done*

## Stage 92 — The submitter may approve their own documents; Send to Vendor without a vendor email (opened + built 2026-10-18 from the user's Approvals and Send to Vendor screenshots)

User, 2026-10-18 (screenshot: My work → Approvals on app.wholeops.in, *"maker-checker violation: you cannot approve or reject a document you submitted"* with the hint *"Correct the field format."*): *"It should allow to me save. ## Stage 93 — A cost center for every location, made with it; postings carry their store's cost center (opened 2026-10-18 from the user's Cost Center screenshot)

User, 2026-10-18 (screenshot: Setup → Cost Center on app.wholeops.in, one row `CostCenter/HQ/2026/000001` "JP Nagar"): *"What is cost center?"* → *"If I have 20 store 1 warehouse for 1 client how many cost center need to create"* → *"Can it create auto cost center once I create a store. A std cost center name should create automatically. It should be editable. Not this lengthy name."* Then (screenshots: Setup → Department `Department/HQ/2026/000001` "Purchase", Setup → Legal Entity `LegalEntity/HQ/2026/000001` "Wholeops" with no GSTIN): *"What is department and Legal Entity. Explain me first than work"* - explained in chat (legal entity = one per GSTIN; department = one per function, not per store; cost center = one per store / warehouse / HO).

Decisions asked 2026-10-18: **every location type** (Store, Warehouse, HO) gets one; **existing locations are linked or given one on release** (a cost center already named like the location is linked, not duplicated); **postings are tagged in the same pass** (the user chose this over backlogging it).

Why it was needed: only journal vouchers and budgets carried a cost center, so every POS sale, invoice and GRN landed under "Unassigned" in the Cost Center P&L. The long code predates 76.5 - CostCenter is a Master, so one made since `840909b` is already `CC00001`; the old record keeps its id (other records may link to it).

| Phase | What | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 93.1 | Location gets a Cost Center field; a new location with none gets one named after it, linked | Built, verified on a scratch instance |
| 93.2 | Existing locations linked or given one on release | Built, verified on a scratch instance |
| 93.3 | Postings tagged with their source document's location's cost center | Built, tested |
| 93.4 | Guides and KB | Built |
| 93.5 | Reaches app.wholeops.in | Blocked on the user's go to push and deploy |
| 93.6 | Cost Center mandatory on a location, suggested ("New: <name>") | Built, verified on a scratch instance |
| 93.7 | Legal Entity mandatory on a location; postings carry the entity | Built, verified on a scratch instance |
| 93.8 | GSTIN mandatory on a legal entity (standard format, no portal check); edits reflect everywhere | Built, verified on a scratch instance |
| 93.9 | Department on every person: on the User; the Employee leads when HR is on | Built, verified on a scratch instance |
| 93.10 | Postings carry the department of the person who raised the document | Built, tested |
| 93.11 | Old long codes cleaned once, guided in Setup (format asked once, preview, renumber) | Built, verified on a scratch instance |

- [ ] **93.1 A new location gets its cost center.** `Location.cost_center` (Link → CostCenter, optional, editable on the location form). Saving a new Store / Warehouse / HO with it blank links an unlinked cost center of the same name, else creates one: name = the location's name, short code from the CostCenter master series (`CC00001`), Active. The name stays editable on Setup → Cost Center; another cost center can be picked on the location. Same rule for the location CSV import. Built: `engines/location_cost_center.go` (`PrepareLocationCostCenter` before the generic save's single write, `EnsureLocationCostCenter` after a CSV import), migration `migrations_stage93_location_cost_center.sql`. `TestLocationCostCenterMadeLinkedAndPosted`; Chrome on a scratch instance: New Location "Jayanagar" → `S00001` with cost center `CC00002` "Jayanagar"; renaming it on Setup → Cost Center showed on the location; the code locked on edit. The Cost Center P&L gained a Name column. *Status: Built, verified on a scratch instance*
- [x] **93.2 Existing locations on release.** Every location with no cost center is linked (same-name, unlinked) or given a new one, once per tenant, idempotent (93.6 superseded "a cleared one is not refilled": a location's cost center is never blank). `BackfillLocationCostCentersAllTenants` runs in `erp-server -migrate` after the schema sync. Fresh-DB migrate: `[cost-center] default: 1 location(s) linked or given a cost center`; a second run did nothing. *Status: Done - on the 0.2.0 deploy (2026-10-19): `[cost-center] default: 2 location(s)`, `minn: 1 location(s) linked or given a cost center`*
- [ ] **93.3 Postings carry the cost center.** A GL posting with no cost center of its own takes the one on its source document's location (POS sale, invoice, GRN, returns, notes...); an Inactive or missing cost center leaves it untagged rather than refusing the posting. A cost center passed explicitly (journal voucher, expense) is unchanged. Built at the one choke point, `PostDoubleEntryTx` (`postingDimensionsTx`, one query inside the posting's transaction): the document's `location` / `location_code` / `return_location` / `store`, else one hop through `sales_invoice_id` / `reference_cart` / `return_request_id` / `grn_id` / `grn_reference` / `employee_id`. *Status: Built, tested*
- [x] **93.4 Guides:** ADMIN_GUIDE B.3.0.3b (the four masters, how many of each, the location's cost center and legal entity, GSTIN, several companies, people's departments, what postings carry, the code clean-up) and the Users bullet; USER_GUIDE (journal voucher cost center, short master codes - the stale `Vendor/HQ/2026/000007` line fixed); KB finance-tax (posting dimensions) and hr-payroll (employee department / location, HR leads the user); REPORT_CATALOG regenerated (Name columns). *Status: Done*

User, 2026-10-18, after the explanation (screenshots: Edit Legal Entity `LegalEntity/HQ/2026/000001` with GSTIN blank; Edit Department `Department/HQ/2026/000001`): *"It should ask Mandatory field and auto linked to the erp's everywhere or all cost center. If I create diff entity. I need solution for this. Dept will be under which store or cost center HO. How to cor-relate dept to Cost Center or Store. How to assign a user to dept? while user creation or Should be part of Employee creation in HR. If HR, IF someone don't take HR than? ... Make it Mandatory those field to fill. Let it be changed. But it should reflect everywhere. If i change. ... So, People should be mapped. I want to mapped from day one automatically. ... Clean them and ask to set them once. Then it should not change code. ... Not good to ask code always. But it should suggest and Mandatory to user."* Answered in chat: a department is not under a store - a person carries a home store (→ cost center) and a department; department on the User so it works without HR, the Employee leads when HR is on.

- [ ] **93.6 Cost Center is mandatory on a location, and suggested.** The New Location form pre-selects *"New: <location name>"* (made on save, 93.1); the user may pick an existing one instead; it is never blank (an edit cannot clear it). Built: `wireLocationCostCenter` (`public/view-pickers.js`) relabels the blank choice and marks the field required; the server fills a blank one on every Location save, create or edit (metadata stays optional so a CSV of stores needs no cost center column). *Status: Built, verified on a scratch instance*

A GL posting with no entity of its own takes its document's location's legal entity, the same choke point as 93.3, so each company has its own trial balance / P&L. Built: the mandatory flag set in every tenant schema by the migration (the schema sync never changes an existing field); `postingDimensionsTx` returns the location's Active legal entity. *Status: Built, verified on a scratch instance*

Built: `LegalEntity.gstin` mandatory in every schema; the existing shape + check-digit rule (Stage 76) is the only check; prints already read the entity live. Not done (noted to the user): an issued tax invoice still reprints with the entity's current GSTIN - freezing it is DEC-17 / 74.x. *Status: Built, verified on a scratch instance*

Built: `users.department`; `engines/person_department.go` (`ResolveDepartmentReference` - id, code or name, Active only, `MASTER-0931`; `SetUserDepartment` refuses a linked user, `MASTER-0932`; `SyncUserDepartmentFromEmployee` on every Employee save); New User asks Department (required, Supplier logins excepted), the Users list shows it with a **Department** button (existing `POST /api/v1/admin/users/location` now also takes `department`); `DepartmentReferenceFields` makes Employee / Expense Claim department a picker resolved to the id, `Employee.location` joins `LocationReferenceFields`, `Employee.department` mandatory; HR screens and the attendance report show the department's name; an expense claim's unknown typed department no longer refuses its payment posting. *Status: Built, verified on a scratch instance*
- [ ] **93.10 Postings carry the department** of the person who raised the document (the creating user's department), unless the document names one. Decided 2026-10-18: yes. Built in `postingDimensionsTx`: the document's own department, else its parent's (a payslip's employee), else the creating user's - not for a journal voucher (booked by accounts for anyone). Department P&L gained a Name column. *Status: Built, tested*

Decided 2026-10-18: guided once in Setup - each master's list asks the format (suggested, mandatory), shows old → new, renumbers on confirm. Built: `engines/master_recode.go` (`PlanMasterRecode` / `ApplyMasterRecode`; one transaction rewrites every whole-value occurrence - text columns, JSON strings and keys, text arrays - and skips the signed audit tables and append-only ledgers; `master_code_changes` keeps each pair and `ResolveLocationReference` reads it for an old store code); `GET` / `POST /api/v1/master-codes/{doctype}/clean` (admin); panel `public/view-master-recode.js` on the four lists. *Status: Built, verified on a scratch instance*

**Stage 93 verification (2026-10-18, scratch databases `custom_erp_t93` / `custom_erp_t93b` on 5490 - created for this, not dropped):** fresh-DB `-migrate` applies cleanly and the backfill is idempotent; `TestLocationCostCenterMadeLinkedAndPosted`, `TestPostingEntityDepartmentAndCodeCleanup` (skips with a reason on a database the migration has not reached), `internal/server` all green on the migrated DB; the full suite's only other failures there were not Stage 93's (`TestStage73CustomerReference` - a sales order now takes a series number; the attack-surface drift from another session's `engines/image_drive.go`). Chrome on the migrated DB, seeded with the user's own shapes: Department / Legal Entity / Location / Cost Center clean-up panels (`DP` asked once and confirmed, the others on their suggestion) → `DP00001` Purchase, `LE00001` Wholeops, `S00001` Jayanagr, `CC00002` JP Nagar - users' departments and home store, the location's legal entity followed; panels gone after. GSTIN blank / wrong check digit held, `29AABCU9603R1ZJ` saved with state code KA; New Location "Indiranagar" showed *Cost Center\* - New: Indiranagar*, was held without a Legal Entity, then made `CC00003`; New User held without a department, then created; the Department button changed cashier1's; a New Employee picked *Sales - DP00002* and *Jayanagr* and its linked user then read *Sales · from HR*. Found and fixed on the way: the two new routes needed `route_capabilities.go` entries (GLOBAL-0011 otherwise); the clean-up hint claimed nothing pointed at a code users did point at; the Employee list showed the department's code (picked plain fields now get the name sweep).

**Stage 93 release steps, in order:** bump `VIEW_MODULE_VERSION` (`view-pickers.js`, `view-documents.js`, `view-users.js`, `view-hr.js` changed; `view-master-recode.js` new); regenerate `docs/security/attack_surface.json` (two new `master-codes/{doctype}/clean` routes - done once 2026-10-18, redo at release since other sessions add routes); `pwsh docs/brain/update-brain.ps1` (three new engine files, one new view module); the deploy's `-migrate` runs the location cost-center backfill after the schema sync - it creates master records on app / minn, so tell the user before the deploy. After the deploy, on each client: Setup → Department / Legal Entity / Location / Cost Center shows the clean-up panel where long codes remain; fill the Legal Entity's GSTIN, give HO a Legal Entity, and give every user a department.

## Stage 94 — Goods receipt in steps: receive, verify, price check into child GRNs, MRP, optional payment; PO design reuse per vendor (opened 2026-10-18 from the user's New goods receipt screenshot)

User, 2026-10-18 (screenshot: Procurement → Goods Receipt → New goods receipt on app.wholeops.in):

> *"In GRN It should ask vendor than it will show pending PO to process. Then it will load selected PO and I will browse the item. I will enter the qty rcv against po qty. Then I will add grn Invoice price ... and Inv Qty ... Then it will go to GRN verification. There will enter GRN Price-(After calling vendor I will revise the Price) and GRN Qty(How much Actually Received in hand, I may receive more or less) Now I will save. Then I will set MRP next screen. Now for new case nothing will happen. If existing item, ERP know the prev purch price. It will match and make a group ... Diff PP or PP Same(Give option to rename). ... Bifurcate in 2 Child grn to process. & Both will go for MRP Validation. Same If Already have MRP populate. Else keep empty to fill. Once saved. GRN will be posted. ... Tell me if i missed something here"*

Then:
- *"I need to type total invoice amount and credit note debit note will generate against our inv amt cal"*
- *"All should be save in table to extract in report and edit is always allowed if user do mistake in any stage."*
- *"Need to capture payment also as accounting. Not mandatory but process should be laid as std and easy way."*
- *"Then in PO, As I told, I need in config, Always New Design number by add or Inherit from existing ... If 1st case create new design else pull from db if exist else create new design and combination id"*

The PO ask is already built and live as **62.6**: Configuration → Procurement → *Design number when a PO adds an item name's design*. The only gap is that the match ignores the vendor (94.13).

**Plan:** [docs/product/grn-receive-verify-mrp-plan-2026-10-18.md](product/grn-receive-verify-mrp-plan-2026-10-18.md). It holds decisions D1-D10 with recommendations, the order of work, and per-phase done-when. D1-D10 were approved as recommended by the user on 2026-10-18.

- [x] **94.0 Decisions.** All ten are proposed in the plan, each with a recommendation. **Approved as recommended (user, 2026-10-18: "All approved which are recommended for me").**
  - D1: stock at GRN price; note = typed invoice total − our total.
  - D2: MRP on the receipt's batch.
  - D3: three child GRNs, each posting on its own.
  - D4: previous price = last GRN price from any vendor.
  - D5: an unexplained total difference above ₹1 must be fixed on the lines.
  - D6: excess above tolerance → Keep (approval) / Return.
  - D7: steps as permissions, plus a quick path.
  - D8: Correct after posting = reverse and re-post the difference.
  - D9: optional Pay now; the vendor bill is made on posting.
  - D10: PO design reuse only within the PO's vendor.

  *Status: Done (decided)*
- [x] **94.1 Line table, steps, correction log, settings.**
  - `grn_line` holds one row per line with every step's figures. The GRN save hook rewrites it, and the document stays the source.
  - `grn_line_change` records field, old → new, step, who and when.
  - Steps: Received → Verified → Priced (per child) → Posted.
  - Settings: excess tolerance, same-price tolerance, blind count, who does which step.

  *Built 2026-10-19:* migration `migrations_stage94_grn_steps.sql` (both tables, plus the `GRNExcessApproval` doctype and its Store Manager approval rule) and `94_2` (`Category.mrp_markup_pct`); `engines/grn_steps.go` (`writeGRNLineRows` from the one save path, `logGRNChanges`, `SyncGRNLineRows` also on every one-step GRN create). A receipt in steps is a GRN with `flow = steps`, status Pending until its last child posts. Settings on Configuration → Procurement: same-price tolerance, blind count, MRP markup / ending, and a role list per step (blank = everyone with the GRN update right; GOODSR-0947). The excess tolerance reuses the existing vendor ReceiptValidationRule rather than a second setting. Tests: `TestStage94GRNSteps` (rows written at Receive, a logged price change). *Status: Done*
- [x] **94.2 Step 1 Receive.**
  - Vendor first; the vendor's open POs as a tick-list; Load.
  - Per line: received qty (typed or scanned), invoice qty and invoice price, defaulting to received qty and PO price.
  - Header: invoice no, date and total (incl. GST), all mandatory. The same vendor + invoice no twice is refused. Invoice photo or PDF attached.
  - Save posts nothing.

  *Built 2026-10-19:* the New goods receipt page: Vendor box → `GET /api/v1/grn-steps/open-pos` tick-list → **Load ticked POs**; Received and Invoice Qty editable on every line; a scan (or Enter) of an item already on the receipt counts one unit with a beep; invoice no / date / total required (GOODSR-0940), duplicate refused (GOODSR-0941); **Save receipt** (`flow: steps`) posts nothing and opens the step page. Over-receipt / not-on-PO are no longer refused at Receive in this flow (decided at Verify). The invoice photo goes on the record's existing Attachments. Found and fixed while walking it: PO-loaded lines had no way to key the received qty on this page. Browser walk 2026-10-19 (:8194). *Status: Done*
- [x] **94.3 Step 2 Verify.**
  - Per line: GRN qty (defaults to received), GRN price (defaults to invoice price), rejected / damaged with reasons.
  - Totals bar: invoice total | our total | the note it will raise, split into price, qty and round-off.
  - Excess / not-on-PO per D6. GST rate vs HSN and IGST vs CGST + SGST check.

  *Built 2026-10-19:* `VerifyGRNStep`; the bar follows the inputs; D5 refusal above ₹1 naming the likely line (GOODSR-0942); Keep → `GRNExcessApproval` through the approval engine (decision hook releases or returns it), Return → added to rejected (GOODSR-0943 when undecided); GST rate vs item HSN rate per line, and same / different state shown. Browser-walked (debit note preview, Keep sent for approval). *Status: Done*
- [x] **94.4 Step 3 Price check and child GRNs.**
  - The previous purchase price (D4) sorts lines into New item / Same price / Price changed (▲/▼, old price, %).
  - Children `GRN-xxxx/A..C`, renamable, lines movable.
  - The vendor invoice stays on the parent.

  *Built 2026-10-19:* `PriceCheckGRNStep`; previous price from `grn_line`, else the latest posted pre-Stage-94 GRN; fixed letters A New / B Same / C Changed; a SKU's lines stay in one child (its stock posts once); moves kept until **Check prices again**. Tested (move and regroup) and browser-walked. *Status: Done*
- [x] **94.5 Step 4 MRP and posting.**
  - Current MRP pre-filled, otherwise empty. Suggested MRP from category markup and price ending; live margin; below-cost / below-current warnings.
  - Save & Post per child: stock at GRN price, batch with MRP, barcodes, GL.
  - When the last child posts: the debit or credit note on invoice total vs our total (Stage 62 note posting, GST per line) and an auto vendor bill.
  - Print stickers per child.

  *Built 2026-10-19:* `PostGRNChildStep` → `PostGRNReceiptPart` (the one-step posting, GL keyed per child); `Batch.mrp` (a same-day per-SKU batch at another MRP gets its own lot); `Item.mrp` = latest; stickers print the lot's MRP, **Print stickers** opens Sticker Printing with that child's lines ticked. Last child: the note (no Stage 62 posting - see the accounting in ADMIN_GUIDE §B.3.6.1: a debit note is applied to the bill as a settlement line, a credit note posts its GST leg) and the vendor bill made and matched. A Purchase Return of the receipt's rejected / damaged units raises no second debit note. Tested to the paisa (2100 and 1500 clear) and browser-walked. *Status: Done*

Cause found: `ComputeGSTForLinesMode` (engines/gst.go) adds up unrounded line amounts and never rounds the totals, so an inclusive-GST PO stores the raw back-calculated taxable value as `total_amount`. Two parts: (a) every derived cost / amount stored and shown at 2 decimals, fixed at the shared GST total (one choke point); (b) MRP and totals rounded to the whole rupee. Decided (user, 2026-10-19): **PO and POS totals** both round to the whole rupee with a round-off line; **MRP is always a whole rupee** (typed or suggested, shown without paise); line costs stay at 2 decimals. *Status: Building*

## Stage 95 — Images for each Design and Combination ID after the PO, stored on each client's own drive (opened 2026-10-18 from the user's message)

User, 2026-10-18:

> *"We have to introduce image upload for Design and Varinat/Combination ID. Once PO doen, I will upload image. We have to build and make it read."*

Then: *"You have suggest where I shoud upload that. Once config need to build to connect for erp and the upload drive. Client to client the url will change."*

What exists: a Design is a `ProductFamily` and each Combination ID is an `Item` under it (`engines/po_design.go`). `ProductMedia` (`engines/pim_media.go`) stores images keyed by item code, on the ERP server's disk (`media_store/`, copied off-box by `backup.sh` media_sync), behind the `pim` module gate. No Go code talks to object storage yet. 75.4.1/75.4.2 planned a platform-wide bucket; this stage needs **one bucket per client**, set in config.

| Phase | What | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 95.0 | Decisions: where files live, where they are uploaded, who sets the drive | Done - approved as recommended (user, 2026-10-18) |
| 95.1 | Per-client image drive settings: endpoint, region, bucket, folder, access key, secret (encrypted in `channel_credentials` as `image_drive`, never shown back); Test connection (write, read back, delete); switching on runs the test first | Done |
| 95.2 | S3 client with hand-written SigV4 (standard library), tested on AWS's published vector and cross-checked against curl's `--aws-sigv4` | Done |
| 95.3 | Every image read and write through one place: upload, file, thumbnail, resized copies, bulk zip, channel publish, print - local or the client's drive, per image | Done |
| 95.4 | Images on a Design (`ProductFamily`): `ProductMedia` with `owner_doctype`, same main / extra rules as an item's | Done |
| 95.5 | PO Images page: the PO's designs, each with its combinations on the PO; add images per design / combination; drop many files, matched by file name (full ID, or the part after the last `/`; `/ _ - space` treated alike; longest match wins); unmatched listed, never dropped; set main; remove | Done |
| 95.6 | The same image strip, with upload, on the Design and Item screens (edit); module-neutral image read routes | Done |
| 95.7 | Tests, error codes PIM-0950..0952, USER_GUIDE / ADMIN_GUIDE, KB, browser walk | Done |
| 95.8 | Release: attack-surface profile copied in, brain redraw, a real provider bucket, commit / deploy | Blocked (user) |
| 95.9 | Design and Combination photo folders set in the Image drive settings (the user's own folders, spaces allowed) | Done |

Scope calls (mine, 2026-10-18): images already on the server stay there and keep working (no copy job - that is 75.4.3); a stored image's thumbnail and resized copies live where the original lives; attachments (`engines/attachments.go`) are not moved; **no public URL field** - the ERP's CSP allows images from itself only, so a CDN URL would need a per-client CSP and a public bucket (75.4.4 stays gated). In this session the user answered D2 with *"After PO"*; read with the approval above as: the PO Images page is the main way in, and the Design / Item screens carry the same uploader.

- [ ] **95.0 Decisions.** Proposed, each with a recommendation (in chat 2026-10-18):
  - D1 where files live: an S3-compatible bucket per client (Cloudflare R2, DigitalOcean Spaces, AWS S3, Backblaze B2, Wasabi, MinIO), with the ERP server's disk as the fallback when a client has no drive set.
  - D2 where images are uploaded: the PO's Images page (from save onwards), and also on the Design and Item screens.
  - D3 who sets the drive: the client's admin in Configuration.

  **Approved as recommended (user, 2026-10-18: "All approved which are recommended for me", given in another session's chat).** *Status: Done (decided)*
- [x] **95.1 Image drive settings.** `engines/image_drive.go`; Configuration → Integrations → **Image drive** (`renderImageDriveSection`, `public/view-images.js`), Super Admin only (`requireHRAdmin`, routes `LevelAdmin`). Blank secret on a later save keeps the stored one; switching on runs the test; every save audited (`IMAGE_DRIVE / SAVED`). **SSRF rule (found in self-review):** https only and a public address only - checked on save (`resolvesToPublicAddressOnly`, the webhook / extension-hook guard) and again at connect time (`imageDriveDial`, no proxy) so a later DNS change cannot reach the server's own network; an on-premise MinIO needs the operator switch `IMAGE_DRIVE_ALLOW_PRIVATE=1`. *Status: Done*
- [x] **95.2 S3 client + SigV4.** `engines/s3_sigv4.go` (standard library). Verified: AWS's published GET Object example gives AWS's exact signature (`TestS3SignRequestAWSExample`), and curl 8.7's own `--aws-sigv4` signature is accepted by a fake bucket that re-signs with ours (`TestStage95SignatureAgreesWithCurl`). Path-style requests, so R2 / Spaces / S3 / B2 / Wasabi / MinIO all fit. Refusals worded per S3 error code (no such bucket, unknown key, wrong secret, access denied, region, clock skew) as PIM-0951. *Status: Done*
- [x] **95.3 One read/write path.** `ReadMediaFile` / `ReadMediaThumbnail` / `ReadMediaTransform` return bytes from disk or the drive (replacing the path-returning `GetMediaFile` / `GetMediaThumbnail` / `GetMediaTransform`); callers moved: both PIM file handlers, transforms, bulk zip, channel publish (`connector.go`), print / Excel item picture (`print_documents.go`). A drive image's record keeps `storage=drive`, `object_key`, `drive_bucket`; thumbnail and resized copies sit beside it on the drive. Bucket layout `<folder>/designs/<design>/<combination>/<sha16>.<ext>`. Image reads and uploads got their own rate-limit buckets (600 / 120 per min) - a 20-line PO's thumbnails would otherwise eat the 60/min every call shares. *Status: Done*
- [x] **95.4 Design images.** `engines/owner_images.go`: `ProductMedia.item` = the design number with `owner_doctype = ProductFamily` (document ids are unique across doctypes). First image is the main one; **Make main** keeps the old main as an extra (a PIM Main Image upload still retires it); removing the main promotes the next; the same file twice is kept once. *Status: Done*

(c) A first real bucket (R2 / Spaces) per client - needs the user's keys; until then every client keeps using the ERP server's disk, exactly as before. *2026-10-18:* user asked to create it in the client Minn's Cloudflare account (screenshot, account `1e44436b…ef953`); no browser control in the session, so the dashboard steps were given in chat - R2 → bucket `minn-erp-images` (private) → Account API token, Object Read & Write on that bucket only → endpoint `https://<account-id>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`, region `auto`. The user enters the keys in Minn's Image drive form after 95.8 (d); the secret is never pasted into chat. **Test plan (user, 2026-10-18):** test on **app.wholeops.in** first, then put the same details in **minn** - on app the same endpoint / bucket / keys with the folders **`app-test/Design Image`** and **`app-test/Combination Image`** (keeps test photos apart in Minn's bucket; delete `app-test` in R2 afterwards), upload on an app PO's Images page, check the files under those folders in the R2 dashboard; then on minn the same details with the user's folders **`Image/Design Image`** and **`Image/Combination Image`** (95.9). The ERP needs three of the R2 token page's values - **S3 API** (endpoint), **Access Key ID**, **Secret Access Key**; the user asked whether the *Token value* is needed (2026-10-18): no - Cloudflare's Access Key ID is the token's id and the Secret Access Key is the SHA-256 of the token value, so the token is already what signs every request; the raw token is for Cloudflare's own management API, which the ERP never calls, and is not stored (an unused secret). Offered to add a field if the user still wants it. (d) **Decided (user, 2026-10-18, asked): wait for the full release** - Stage 95 ships with the other sessions' stages (89, 92, 93, modules 3-5), not on its own with the three unpushed commits. Commit / deploy when the user says. No migration. *Status: Blocked (user)*
- [ ] **95.9 Design and Combination folders in the Image drive settings.** User, 2026-10-18: *"I have created two folder for combination and design number. So, After keys and everything new a config in UI to setup the design image upload of erp in design folder path and same for combination"*, then the two folders: `minn-erp-images/Image/Combination Image/` and `minn-erp-images/Image/Design Image/`. Build: the single optional **Folder** is replaced by two required fields, **Design images folder** and **Combination images folder** (spaces allowed); a design's photos go to `<Design folder>/<Design ID>/`, every item's (combination's) photos to `<Combination folder>/<Combination ID>/` - no more nesting combinations under their design. Test connection writes, reads back and deletes a test file in **each** folder. Photos already stored keep their full key, so changing the folders later never loses them. Built: `ImageDriveSettings.DesignFolder` / `CombinationFolder` (`tidyImageFolder` trims each part; letters, digits, spaces, `. _ - ( )`; `..` refused), `s3Client.ownerKey`, `checkFolder` per folder; the item→design lookup for the key is gone. Verified: Stage 95 engine tests with the user's exact folder names (spaces; required / malformed folders refused by name; design and combination photos land in their folders and read back), curl's SigV4 accepted on a path with a space, and a Chrome walk - form saved with `Image/Design Image` / `Image/Combination Image`, the test file written and deleted in both, a design photo stored at `Image/Design Image/Design-HO-26-27-000005/…` and a combination photo at `Image/Combination Image/Design-HO-26-27-000005-Red-S/…`. ## Stage 98 — Print, PDF, send and export for every transaction: PDF download, the vendor's copy sent by the ERP, the right printer at each store chosen once, CSV / Excel line exports with the item matrix, attribute names you can rename (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's Send to Vendor screenshot and two messages)

User, 2026-10-19 (screenshot: the *Sent to Vendor* pop-up on a PO): *"I want to download in vendor template and Sent it to Vendor. Same for PO, GRN, TO and Sales Bill. I want a pdf download and print as well. As we have print set up. It will call diff printer for barcode and other a4 or a5 or pos bill style other style prin t automatically if all connected. ERP will be installed in 100 stores. All printer will be different. Need auto call to those printer. once erp connected. It should ask one time which printer for what txn once then nver ask. But user can change it. & I am ok if it happen automatically with out asking user in erp."* Then: *"i can download csv & excel also each txn where ever product invovled as design combination barcode qty and all other item matrixes and mrp (& add cost price in po and grn only.) & other detail as per txn po number, or grn number, vendor name & I need brand in item matrix. (Optional) User can rename atribute name like color to colour or anything."*

**What exists (found while planning, 2026-10-19).**
- The Print dialog (`printTransactionDocument`, `public/trade-docs.js`) on PO (PO / Vendor PO), GRN (GRN / Vendor Copy), Transfer (Out / In), Sales Invoice, the POS tax invoice, returns and notes: template, generating store, live preview. **Print** is the browser's print dialog (`window.print()`). **Excel** exists only for a PO (`/purchase-order/{id}/excel`, the hand-written `engines/xlsx_writer.go`, no library). No PDF download anywhere.
- **Send to Vendor** stamps `sent_to_vendor_at` and fires `PurchaseOrderIssued` at a `NotificationChannelConfig` webhook: nothing is attached and the ERP emails nobody itself. The server can already send mail (`net/smtp` with `SMTP_HOST` ..., used for account notices). The blocking pop-up in the screenshot is already a toast in the tree (92.4).
- **Silent printing** exists through QZ Tray (`public/qz-print.js`) for stickers, shipping labels and receipts. `resolveQZPrinter` (`internal/server/handlers_qz_print.go`) takes the first Active Printer whose *Default For* matches the job **across the whole company**: the Printer's Location is ignored, so 100 stores would all get the same printer. *Default For* knows Shipping Label, Invoice, Sticker, Receipt, General - not PO, GRN or Transfer. A4 / A5 documents never go through QZ.
- Every print is built by one function, `BuildPrintDocument` (`engines/print_documents.go`): each line already carries SKU, barcode, design, variant, qty, rate, MRP, tax, amount and every attribute under its Item Attribute **Label** (87). An export built on it covers every transaction at once.
- Item Attribute has a code and a **Label**; the label is what the PO add row, the prints and the 87 columns show. Industry-profile fields (the jewellery Color, Polish ...) are relabelled through Settings ▸ Customize ▸ Dynamic Labels.

**Decisions (asked 2026-10-19; answered the same day: D1 picture of the print, D2 both - the ERP emails the PDF and a PDF download is always there to send yourself, D3 per computer on the server, D5 as recommended; D4 automatic, as the user allowed; D6 and D7 as recommended):**
- **D1 How the PDF is made.** Recommended: from the pages the print preview already shows - each page drawn to an image in the browser and put into a PDF by a small hand-written writer (no library). Print, PDF, email and silent print then look the same; the PDF is a picture of the page (text not selectable, about 0.3-0.5 MB a page). Alternative: a PDF writer in Go on the server - selectable text, but a second layout engine to keep identical to the template studio, and a font to ship for the ₹ sign. A JavaScript PDF library is ruled out by the no-dependency rule.
- **D2 How Send reaches the vendor.** Recommended: the ERP emails it itself - to the vendor's email, the PDF attached, sent from the server's mail account with the store's email as Reply-To; the webhook keeps firing for anyone who uses one. With no vendor email it downloads the PDF and records the send, as now.
- **D3 Where "which printer for what" is kept.** Recommended: per computer, saved on the server - computer + store + kind of print → printer, so it survives a browser reset and can be seen centrally; a store-wide Printer record (Location + Default For) is the fallback for a new computer. Alternative: per store only - simpler, but two billing counters in one store could not use different printers.
- **D4 Ask or choose automatically.** The user allowed automatic. Planned: choose by itself when the choice is clear (A4 / A5 documents → the computer's default printer; bills → the one receipt printer; stickers → the one label printer), ask once only when two printers fit; a toast names the printer used, with **Change**.
- **D5 Prices in exports.** Recommended: PO and GRN carry cost (rate, taxable, tax, amount); a sales bill carries its own selling price (it is printed on the bill); transfers, returns and every other export carry **MRP only**.
- **D6 Brand.** Recommended: a **Brand** column in exports and in the print's item table, filled from the item; shown when any line has a brand, and a template or export can leave it out.
- **D7 Renaming attribute names.** Recommended: the **Label** on Setup ▸ Inventory ▸ Item Attribute is the one name shown everywhere (PO, GRN, prints, exports, filters); renaming never changes stored values, because the code stays.

**Order:** 98.1 exports (no device work) → 98.2 PDF → 98.3 send → 98.4 printer routing → 98.5 docs and walk-through.

**Build status** (statuses: Not started · Building · Built, not verified · Done · Needs decision · Blocked):

## Stage 99 — Shopify: products, prices and stock out; orders, payments and refunds in; shipping sent back (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's message)

User, 2026-10-19: *"Shopify Integration. Data Custom Dasboard. CRM Moule better/best one. Store app. Add in checklist to build all this"* - recorded as one Stage each: **99** Shopify, **100** custom data dashboards, **101** CRM, **102** store app. Nothing built yet. Suggested order across the four: 100 first (no outside dependency, and 101 and 102 read its datasets) → 101 → 102; 99.1-99.7 can be built at any time against a fake Shopify in tests, only the live run (99.8) waits for credentials.

**What exists (found while recording, 2026-10-19).**
- `engines/connector_shopify.go` publishes one product with its images (GraphQL Admin API, staged uploads) and reads it back; its rate limit self-corrects from Shopify's query cost. The publish payload (`ChannelProductPayload`, `engines/connector.go`) is product-level - title, description, mapped attributes, images - with no variants, prices or stock.
- `POST /api/v1/integration/shopify/product/map` links an ERP SKU to Shopify; `POST /api/v1/integration/shopify/order` takes the orders webhook (HMAC-checked) into a Sales Order with customer name, shipping address and SKU / qty / price only - no Shopify order number, discounts, shipping charge, tax lines, payment, phone or email.
- The token and shop address are stored encrypted (`engines/channel_credentials.go`).
- Under the Stage 35.6 connector SDK, Shopify is a **legacy** connector (`engines/connector_zz_legacy_sdk.go`): pull orders, push stock and push order status are not implemented. WooCommerce has all three and is the pattern. So stock, prices, shipping / tracking, cancellations, refunds and payouts do not flow today.
- It has never run against a real Shopify store: 20.3 / 26.2.1 have waited for non-production credentials since 2026-07.

**Decisions (asked 2026-10-19; not yet answered):**
- **D1 Which side owns what.** Recommended: the ERP owns products, prices and stock; Shopify owns online orders and their payments. A price or stock edit made in Shopify admin is overwritten at the next sync, and the sync log says so.
- **D2 How the ERP connects.** Recommended: a Shopify **custom app** in each client's store, its admin token pasted once into the ERP. Alternative: a public Shopify app (one-click install for every client) - needs a Shopify Partner account, Shopify's app review and an install (OAuth) flow.
- **D3 Stock per location.** Recommended: each Shopify location mapped to one ERP store or warehouse; the quantity sent is available minus reserved minus a safety buffer (a setting), never below 0.
- **D4 How orders arrive.** Recommended: webhooks (order created / updated / cancelled, refund created) plus a pull every 15 minutes that catches any webhook Shopify failed to deliver; the Shopify order ID stops an order being imported twice.
- **D5 Which store ships.** Recommended: the store mapped to the Shopify location Shopify chose; the OMS allocation rules move it when that store has no stock.
- **D6 Shopify POS.** Recommended: out of scope - the ERP's own POS bills the stores.

**Needed from the user:** a Shopify development store (or the client's store), a custom app on it with access to products, inventory, locations, orders, fulfilments and customers, and its webhook secret.

**Order:** 99.1 connector (built against Shopify's API shape in tests, no store needed) → 99.2 setup page → 99.3 products and prices → 99.4 stock → 99.5 orders → 99.6 shipping, cancellations, refunds → 99.7 payouts → 99.8 live run on a development store → 99.9 guides.

## Stage 100 — Custom data dashboards: build your own charts and number tiles from the ERP's data, filtered by date and store (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's message)

User, 2026-10-19: *"Data Custom Dasboard"* (one of four asks; see Stage 99's opening).

**What exists (found while recording, 2026-10-19).**
- The home dashboard is a saved **DashboardLayout** (`engines/dashboard.go`, 37.11): a list of tiles, each tile one registered report shown as its table; private, or the default for a role. **DashboardDigest** emails all its tiles as one CSV on a schedule. The default is four exception reports.
- Reports come from the `ReportDefinition` / `RegisterReport` framework, already cut by the viewer's store scope and money permission.
- Fixed per-module screens: warehouse cockpit, PIM dashboard, OMS console tiles, CRM analytics (lifetime value, cohort retention). The Inventory dashboard (88.5.1) is not started.
- No charts anywhere: the front end draws none, and a tile cannot be a number, a line or a bar.

**Decisions (asked 2026-10-19; not yet answered):**
- **D1 What a widget can read.** Recommended: (a) any registered report, and (b) a **dataset** builder over a fixed list of datasets - sales lines, POS bills, stock on hand, stock movements, purchase orders, goods receipts, transfers, customers, loyalty, online orders, expenses, ledger - pick fields, filters, group by, sum / count / average. The server builds the query from that list only, with the viewer's store scope and money permission applied. Rejected: a SQL box, even for admins (another client's data, the database's safety).
- **D2 Widget types.** Recommended: number with change against the previous period, line, bar (stacked too), donut, table and pivot (rows × columns), drawn as SVG by a small hand-written helper - no chart library (first principle).
- **D3 Filters.** Recommended: dashboard-wide date (today, this week, month to date, custom), store, brand, category and channel; a widget may fix its own.
- **D4 Who builds, who sees.** Recommended: a **Build dashboards** permission; share with users, roles or stores; every viewer sees only their own stores' data, and money only if their role may, so one dashboard serves every store manager.
- **D5 How fresh.** Recommended: loaded on open and refreshed every 5 minutes while open (a setting); each widget's result kept for 60 seconds on the server so 100 stores opening the same dashboard do not each hit the database.

**Order:** 100.1 data layer → 100.2 charts and the widget editor → 100.3 dashboards, sharing, ready-made set → 100.4 guides and walk-through.

## Stage 101 — CRM made best-in-class: one customer page, segments, campaigns that really send, automatic journeys, feedback, and a sales pipeline for business buyers (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's message; planned the same day from the user's CRM blueprint PDF)

User, 2026-10-19: *"CRM Moule better/best one"* (one of four asks; see Stage 99's opening).

**What exists (found while recording, 2026-10-19).**
- CRM menu (88.1.2): Customers · Loyalty · Campaigns · Vouchers · Service Tickets.
- Customers: phone-first at the till (85), merge duplicates, the Customer 360 report, the customer card at the till (73.3.2), customer advance.
- Loyalty: earn and burn, tiers with benefits and grace (73.3.1), point adjustments with approval (73.2.11), points as a liability (73.2.12), reversal on returns.
- Campaigns and Vouchers, issued vouchers taken at the till (73.2.10); marketing consent per channel - SMS, WhatsApp, email (`engines/marketing_consent.go`).
- Analytics: lifetime value, cohort retention, CleverTap segments received (`engines/crm_analytics.go`).
- Service Tickets and Service Contracts (`public/view-service-desk.js`).
- **Messages do not reach customers by themselves**: each channel posts to a webhook the client must connect to their own SMS / WhatsApp / email sender (`engines/notifications.go`) - a 2026-07-25 choice made to avoid provider libraries.
- Missing: leads, opportunities, follow-up tasks, one timeline per customer, a segment builder, automatic journeys (birthday, lapsed ...), feedback / NPS.

**The blueprint (2026-10-19).** The user gave *CRM Platform Business and Product Requirements* v1.0 (`Downloads\MyBusiness\IT Solution\ERP\Docs\PDF\CRM_Platform_BRD_PRD_Master_Blueprint.pdf`, 70 pages, 38 modules M01-M38 in phases P0-P2) with *"Indepth CRM Module ... Understand it and plan it and put it in checklist and relevant places."* It is the detailed spec for this stage. Plan, decisions and what is not adopted: `docs/product/crm-platform-plan-2026-10-19.md`. Items below keep that file's IDs; blueprint module IDs in brackets. Plan not yet approved.

**Decisions** (full table and recommendations in the plan, section 1):
- **D1 Which CRM first** - rec. retail first, business pipeline last (the blueprint agrees). *Needs decision*
- **D2 Real sending** - rec. email via the store mail account (98.3) + one DLT SMS provider for the pilot, WhatsApp Cloud API next; webhook kept. Needs the user's accounts, DLT IDs, templates. *Needs decision*
- **D3 Benchmark** - settled by the blueprint's research (CleverTap, Braze, Segment, HubSpot, Customer.io, six open-source CRMs).
- **D4 Store staff** - rec. store app (102.4.1) + clienteling worklist (101.12). *Needs decision*
- **D5 One phone, one customer** - kept (85 D1); the blueprint's shared-phone profiles not adopted.
- **D6 Consent scope** - rec. per client × channel × purpose (marketing / service); per brand only for multi-brand clients. *Needs decision*
- **D7 No recorded consent = not reachable** - settled (55.17a).
- **D8 Contact limits** - rec. 2 per 24 h, 5 per 7 days, 4 h apart, none 21:00-09:00; per-client settings. *Needs decision*
- **D9 Journey editor** - rec. step list with yes / no branches; canvas later. *Needs decision*
- **D10 Campaign credit** - rec. last marketing message within 7 days before the bill, once per bill, returns reduce it; hold-outs for lift. *Needs decision*
- **D11 Points on returnable bills** - rec. keep earn-now-reverse-on-return; "pending until the return window ends" as a per-programme option. *Needs decision*
- **D12 Retention** - rec. messages 24 months, consent evidence for the customer's life, hashed suppression after erasure. *Needs decision*
- **D13 Campaign approval** - rec. the approval engine with the client's Stage 92 self-approval setting, plus a campaign-only "second approver" override. *Needs decision*

**Order:** 101.0 → 101.1 customer page → 101.8 consent → 101.2 segments → 101.9 send gate → 101.3 templates, senders, campaigns (**pilot gate**: one real email + SMS campaign reconciled) → 101.4 journeys → 101.5 feedback and service → 101.11 loyalty and offers → 101.12 clienteling → 101.3.5 hold-outs → 101.6 business pipeline → 101.7 throughout, closes the stage.

## Stage 102 — Store app: an app on the phone or tablet for store staff - today's sales, approvals, scan an item, receive, count, print a label, serve a customer (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's message)

User, 2026-10-19: *"Store app"* (one of four asks; see Stage 99's opening). Earlier, 2026-10-18 (55.5a): *"we will build a PWA for the approval"*.

**What exists (found while recording, 2026-10-19).**
- The ERP is a web app laid out for a desktop or till screen: no app manifest, no service worker, nothing to install on a phone.
- The POS keeps selling offline: sales queue in the browser and sync when the line returns, with a heartbeat and a gap check (20.13; POS Offline Gaps).
- Pieces a store app would reuse: store access per user and the working store (85), the approval engine and the task inbox (73.3.3), scan-to-age lookup (61), the RF traceability screens (`public/view-rf-traceability.js`), sticker printing through QZ Tray (desktop only), and the second approval for returns, built and switched off (55.5a).

**Decisions (asked 2026-10-19; D1, D2 and D5 answered the same day - user, replying to "for store staff rather than shoppers? an installable web app with no app store and no new framework? phone billing stays out of the first version": *"We can build a pwa"* - all three as recommended. D3 and D4 not yet answered):**
- **D1 Who it is for.** **Decided: store staff** - manager, cashier, salesman, stock staff. A shopping app for the brand's customers is a different product (Shopify's own mobile store, with Stage 99, covers online buying).
- **D2 How it is built.** **Decided: an installable web app (PWA)** served by the same server at `/store/`, plain JavaScript like `public/` - installed from the browser, no app store, updated on next open; a Play Store listing later by wrapping the same app (TWA) if wanted. Rejected: a native, Flutter or React Native app (a new framework and build step; first principle).
- **D3 Offline.** Recommended: item, price and stock lookups work from the last download; counts and scans queue offline and sync later; approvals need a connection. *Until answered:* the app itself opens offline and says it is offline; lookups and approvals need a connection.
- **D4 Signing in on a shared phone.** Recommended: the manager registers the phone to the store once; each person then signs in with their own PIN, locked after a few idle minutes (49.14.3). *Until answered:* the app uses the ERP's own sign-in (password, then the two-factor code where the role needs it; the Stage 67 shift session) - right for a personal phone; the PIN is added on top once decided, with no rework.
- **D5 Billing on the phone.** **Decided: not in the first cut** - a salesman builds the basket on the phone and the till recalls it (77's hold / resume); full billing on a tablet later if asked.

**Order:** 102.1 the app shell → 102.2 manager → 102.3 stock → 102.4 selling floor → 102.5 guides and real-phone checks. **First slice (started 2026-10-19):** the parts that need no other stage - 102.1.1 the installable app, 102.1.2 camera scanning, 102.2.2 approvals and 102.3.1 scan an item. 102.2.1 waits for Stage 100's datasets, 102.3.4 for 98.4, 102.4.1 for 101.1.

## Stage 104 — The Control Panel's "36 errors" for the default client: read, explained, fixed (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's Control Panel screenshot)

User, 2026-10-19: *"36 error are there. Read and fix."* (Settings → Control Panel, Default ERP Client, Errors (24h) = 36, last 19 Oct 12:03 am.)

Read from production by the user (`tenant_default.system_error_logs`, last 30 hours; prod reads are blocked for the agent). None of the 36 was a server fault - every row was a catalog message a user was shown, logged because every catalog row is "log required":

| n | Code | What it was | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| 24 | REPORT-0160 | A report ran and had no rows for its filters (a normal 200 answer) | Not an error - 104.1 |
| 6 | GLOBAL-0001 | PO edit refused "PO Number (Internal) is required" (10-17) | Real bug, **still live on 840909b**: the PO screen's edit save (`poDraftPayload`) sends no `code` / `po_number` and the deployed generic save replaces the record. Fixed by 73.D.8's merge in `57d3d04` - ships with the next release |
| 4 + 1 | GLOBAL-0006 / GLOBAL-0002 | Industry "locked" / "Unknown industry_code" (10-18 00:55-01:38, build 0845470) | The old top-bar industry picker posted without `override` first by design, then retried with a reason; replaced by Configuration → Industry (sends the override). Nothing to fix |
| 4 + 1 | GLOBAL-0002 / META-0198 | Self-approval refused; a PO's vendor not usable (10-18 13:07-14:16) | The user's own ask - Stage 92 self-approval in `57d3d04`, ships with the next release |
| 3 + 2 | GLOBAL-0004 / GLOBAL-0019 | Record not found / status not allowed (10-17) | Not traceable: the log row did not say which request - 104.3 |
| 2 | GLOBAL-0009 | Session expired | Expected |

| Phase | What | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 104.1 | An empty report is not logged as an error | Done |
| 104.2 | Errors (24h) and the error-rate alert count faults, not user rejections | Done |
| 104.3 | Each logged message names its request | Done |
| 104.4 | Tests, guide, trackers | Done (KB HTML regenerates at release) |

Not committed, not deployed: prod shows the change after the next release (old rows are re-read, so the 36 drop to 0 then; the PO edit fix rides on `57d3d04`, already committed).

- [x] **104.1 An empty report is not logged as an error.** `handleRunReport` logged REPORT-0160 on every report with no rows, although its own comment calls that a normal, non-blocking 200 answer. Keep the code / message annotation on the response; drop the log write. *Status: Done* - `internal/server/handlers_report_engine.go`; `TestEmptyReportIsNotLoggedAsError` (200 + REPORT-0160 note, no row for its correlation id).
- [x] **104.2 Errors (24h) and the sustained-error-rate alert count faults only.** A row whose message starts with a catalog code the catalog answers with 2xx / 4xx (a request the user can correct) is a rejection, not a fault; worker errors, panics and 5xx answers still count. Severity cannot tell them apart (163 High / Critical codes are 4xx rejections such as GOODSR-0096). `internal/server` hands the code list to `engines` (which cannot import the catalog); the Control Panel count + its "last" time and `checkErrorRate` filter on it. Rows stay in the log (Activity Log → System Errors, correlation-id lookup). Applies to old rows too, so prod's 36 read 0 after the release. *Status: Done* - `engines.ClientErrorCodes` / `systemFaultSQL` (`engines/logs.go`), set lazily from the catalog in `internal/server/apierror.go` (an extension row added by another init is included); `engines/platform_control_panel.go`, `engines/alerting.go`. Unset (engine-only tests) counts every row, as before. Tests: `TestClientErrorCodesSplitRejectionsFromFaults` (every code has the `[A-Z]+-[0-9]+` shape the SQL reads; the 36's codes are rejections, GLOBAL-0302 / 0010 / 0308, REPORT-0162 stay faults), `TestSystemFaultSQLLeavesOutRejections` (5 seeded rows → 3 faults, a High 4xx code excluded).
- [x] **104.3 Each logged catalog message names its request** (`METHOD /path`, no query string; no route carries a secret in its path). *Status: Done* - `logForEntry` appends ` | GET /api/v1/...`; `TestLoggedCatalogErrorNamesItsRequest`.
- [x] **104.4 Tests, ADMIN_GUIDE (Control Panel Errors column), ledger, handover.** *Status: Done* - also `docs/operations/incident_runbook.md` and `docs/kb/admin-operations/incident-response.md` (the error-rate trigger); `internal/kb/content` HTML regenerates with `update-docs.ps1` at release (not run now: it would bake other sessions' unfinished guide edits in). Plus `TestStage104POEditWithoutNumberFieldsSaves` in `generic_save_merge_stage73d8_test.go`: a PO edit shaped like the PO screen's (no `code` / `po_number`) saves at HEAD; with the 73.D.8 merge disabled it fails with exactly the production message (GLOBAL-0001, "PO Number (Internal)" (code)). Verified: build, vet, targeted engine tests, full `internal/server` package (103 s, ok).

- [x] **108.1 New Location fills itself in.** One standard Short Code rule: an abbreviation word (JP, MG) is kept whole, other words give their first letter, topped up to 3 from the last word, at most 4 letters, a digit added on a clash ("JP Nagar" → JPN). Facility Code = highest numeric one + 1 (1, 2, 3...). Facility Level gains HO and Store and follows Type (Store → Store, Warehouse → Warehouse, HO → HO). Sellable follows Type (Store → Yes, else No). Parent = the HO location (blank for an HO). Legal Entity = the first one (changeable when there are two). Ask the salesman at the till = Mandatory. All editable; a field the user changes stops following. Existing locations are not touched. *Built 2026-10-19:* `wireNewLocationDefaults` (view-pickers.js, create only), `engines/location_identity.go` (`SuggestLocationShortCode`, `PrepareNewLocationCodes`, called on create in handlers_core_doc_engine.go before the cost center), `db/migrations_stage108_location_level_types.sql` (level options gain HO and Store, only where still the shipped list), ADMIN_GUIDE B.3.0.3a. `TestSuggestLocationShortCode`, `TestPrepareNewLocationCodes` and the Stage 93 cost-center test pass (5490 scratch DB, migration applied there by hand).
