## Stage 109 — One click to release, one click to deploy, the server cleans itself; SSH bots blocked (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's message)

User, 2026-10-19: *"I want auto mechanism to do this ... What you are doing for push to github. I want a mechanism what are you checking a script or bot to build for me to do all that ... It should learn parallely with you doing in future. Once you say all loophole close, I will run that ... Next for deployment in server and Clean up of server ... Three action three click. 3rd one if can be done without me also fine. Like a scheduler in erp or server."* Then, on the SSH note: *"How to fix this"*.

**Decisions (made here, from the user's "tell the suitable way"):** D1 the release and deploy are PowerShell scripts in the repo started by double-click `.cmd` files at the root, like `Open-ERP.cmd` - no new tools or dependencies. D2 the cleanup runs on the server as a root cron job, not inside the ERP: it needs root, must work when the ERP is down, and keeps the ERP light. D3 the deploy click shows every pending migration that changes client data and waits for the user to type YES - it replaces Claude's questions before a deploy. D4 work that must not ship is listed in `deploy/release-hold.txt` (git-ignored); the release leaves those paths out. D5 the gates live in one script plus `deploy/RELEASE_GATES.md`; a session that meets a release step the script missed adds it there in the same session (CLAUDE.md rule). D6 SSH bots: sshguard (C, ~2 MB RAM), not fail2ban (Python, ~30 MB) and not ufw `limit` (a deploy opens ~9 SSH connections in under a minute and would lock itself out).

| Phase | Items | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 109.1 Release click | 1 | Done |
| 109.2 Deploy click | 1 | Done |
| 109.3 Server cleanup on its own | 1 | Done |
| 109.4 The gates learn | 1 | Done |
| 109.5 Server slimmed once | 1 | Done |
| 109.6 SSH bots | 1 | Done |
| 109.7 One release number everywhere | 1 | Done |
| 109.8 One folder for the clicks | 1 | Done |
| 109.9 Release in ~9 minutes, not 15 | 1 | Done |
| 109.10 Faster, never less accurate | 1 | Done |
| 109.11 Droplet matches the repo | 1 | Done |

- [x] **109.1 `Release.cmd` → `deploy/release.ps1`.** Everything the 2026-10-18/19 releases did by hand, in order: preflight (on main, index empty, not behind origin, tree quiet for 10 min, Postgres up), snapshot into a `%TEMP%` worktree minus held paths, LF for text files only (never binaries, never the `i/mixed` manifests), gofmt where HEAD was clean, view-module / stylesheet `?v=` bumps, catalog spec, attack surface, KB, graphify + brain (an unclaimed file goes to the region of its nearest sibling), document register, build, vet, `node --check`, release budget, a data-change scan, fresh-DB migrate + full tests (failed tests rerun twice - pass = flaky, listed), commit through the shared index only if the tree did not move, sync regenerated files back, push, report. `-DryRun` stops before the commit. *Status: Done - first live run 2026-10-19: release 0.5.0, commit 7ca4d4e, every gate green, pushed. The sandbox run (throwaway origin) found two bugs first: piping into `git update-index` re-encodes (now `--cacheinfo` arguments), and the helper's output reached filters as one block (now parenthesised)*
- [x] **109.2 `Deploy.cmd` → `deploy/deploy_release.ps1`.** Refuses a commit that is not on origin or has no passed release report; lists the pending migrations on prod that write data and asks for YES; backup + sha256 check, rollback copy, deploy from a clean worktree of the commit, both hosts checked (`/`, health, version = commit, `app.js?v=`), then a cleanup pass. `-Check` is read-only. *Status: Done - first live run 2026-10-19: 0.5.0 (7ca4d4e) on app and minn, backup sha256 OK, rollback copy, both hosts 200 with the new build, cleanup pass pruned one rollback copy. Found: the ledger note read "version set by hand" because `$Notes` and `$notes` are one variable in PowerShell - the parameter is now `-ReleaseNote` (ships next release)*
- [x] **109.3 `deploy/server_cleanup.sh` + `install_cleanup_cron.sh`.** Root cron at 03:30 (after the 02:00 backup), installed and refreshed by every deploy: keep the newest 3 rollback copies, delete failed-deploy binaries older than 7 days and stray control-character file names, stale `*.new`, `apt-get clean`, journal held at 100 MB, failed-login log trimmed, the snapd pin kept; alert through `OPS_ALERT_WEBHOOK_URL` above 80% disk; one line a run in `/var/log/erp-cleanup.log`. Never touches backups, the live or previous build, the database. `Cleanup.cmd` runs it on demand. *Status: Done - cron installed on the box 2026-10-19 (`30 3 * * *`), rules tested on a seeded scratch folder there, first run logged*
- [x] **109.4 The gates learn.** `deploy/RELEASE_GATES.md` (each gate, the incident that taught it, how to add one), the CLAUDE.md rule, the hold file. *Status: Done - README Part F0 gate table, CLAUDE.md rule, hold file*
- [x] **109.5 Server slimmed once (2026-10-19, on the user's "All of it").** Disk 66% → 27%: journald capped at 100 MB, apt caches cleared, snapd + lxd / core20 / core22 purged and pinned out, swap 2 GB → 512 MB, old failed-deploy binaries and a stray `erp-server\r` deleted, 23 old rollback copies pruned (newest 3 kept). ERP, Postgres, Caddy active; both hosts 200 on 0.4.0. *Status: Done*
- [x] **109.6 SSH bots blocked (2026-10-19, user chose sshguard).** SSH was already key-only (no password or keyboard login, root by key only), so the 40,824 failed logins this month (14,660 from one address) were noise, not a risk. sshguard installed: an address is blocked for an hour after ~3 failures, remembered for a day, and blocked for good after repeat offences (`/var/lib/sshguard/enemies`); localhost whitelisted; key logins and deploys never count. *Status: Done*
- [x] **109.7 One release number everywhere** (user, 2026-10-19: *"in prod is release. update everywhere"*). Production showed 0.4.0 while `internal/server/VERSION` (an unstamped build, the manuals' `release:` line) and the ADMIN_GUIDE examples still said 0.1.0 - deploy.ps1 worked the number out at deploy time and stamped only the binary. Now release gate G4b reads the live number, applies cmd/releasever's rule (feat → MINOR, docs only → PATCH) and writes VERSION before the docs are regenerated; the commit subject starts `feat:` / `docs:` to match; Deploy.cmd passes that number to deploy.ps1 (`-Version`, new `-ReleaseNote`). ADMIN_GUIDE C.2 examples made generic and the rule restated. *Status: Done - 0.5.0 is the number in production, `internal/server/VERSION`, the manuals and the ledger (2026-10-19)*
- [x] **109.8 One folder for the clicks, easy to reach** (user, 2026-10-19: *"make one folder for them. In proper std way"*, *"easy for me to access as well"*). Everything moved into a top-level `release/`: the three clicks at its top (`Release.cmd`, `Deploy.cmd`, `Cleanup.cmd`), `README.md` (how to use + the gate table, moved from `deploy/README.md` Part F0, which now points to it), `config.json`, `hold.txt` and `reports/` (both git-ignored), and `scripts/` with PowerShell Verb-Noun names (`Invoke-Release.ps1`, `Invoke-Deploy.ps1`, `Invoke-Cleanup.ps1`, `ReleaseCommon.ps1`). The server-side `server_cleanup.sh` / `install_cleanup_cron.sh` stay in `deploy/`, which mirrors `/opt/erp/deploy` on the box. Brain map claims `release/*`; CLAUDE.md and the handover point at the new paths. *Status: Done - Cleanup.cmd dry run, Deploy -Check and a full Release dry run all passed from release/ (2026-10-19)*
- [x] **109.9 Release in about 5 minutes, not 15** (user, 2026-10-19: *"Why it takes 15 mins?"*). The tests ran one package at a time on one database (engines alone 5-9 min) while 7 of 8 cores sat idle. Now each test job gets its own copy of the freshly migrated database (`CREATE DATABASE ... TEMPLATE`) and up to 4 run side by side: engines in 3 groups (compiled once, `-test.run` per group), internal/server, then the small packages; 4 at most because a test process may open 50 connections and local Postgres allows 100. The code graph builds while the KB is generated. A docs-only release runs only the docs packages (also when commits since origin hold no code). Failed tests still rerun alone on their copy. The commit subject names the checklist items when no ledger section is added (0.6.0 said "39 file(s)"). *Status: Done - dry run 2026-10-19: 9:22 in all (was ~15), the tests 3:57 (was 7-9 min: server 2:21, engines groups 3:12 / 3:42 / 3:57, small packages 3:44), every scratch database dropped after. Gate headings now show the elapsed time so the slow gates (generators, build, vet) can be tuned next. Ships with the next release*
- [x] **109.10 Faster again, never less accurate** (user, 2026-10-19: *"1st can we make more faster?"*, then *"But faster doesn't mean inaccuracy and it should always deliver correctly"*, *"1st push then deploy"*). Faster: build, vet, the budget and both test programs compile side by side (G8); one doclint run; engines groups balanced by each test's time in the last run (`release/reports/test-times.json`); the small packages run beside the four database-heavy jobs; gate headings show elapsed time. Reviewed against accuracy - kept only what cannot let a bad release through, and tightened: the docs-only test shortcut and the catalog-generator skip are **reverted** (a docs change can break server tests; a stale generated file committed outside Release must still be caught); running test binaries directly would have skipped 2 engines examples / fuzz seeds - they are now listed and run like `go test`; every group must report a result for every test it was given, or the release stops; more than 3 flaky tests stops it. *Status: Done - live 2026-10-19: release 0.7.0 (f7acbdd) pushed and deployed; all 777 engines + server tests, examples and fuzz seeds reported a result; groups balanced 213 s / 213 s / 213 s, finishing within 23 s of each other; tests 3:33, whole Release 9:40 (the accuracy checks spend what the speed-ups saved). The first live try stopped in G8 before committing: a job name with a "/" made a bad log path - fixed and rerun*
- [x] **109.11 Everything on the droplet matches the repo** (user, 2026-10-19: *"all updated in droplet now?"*). Checked file by file against release 0.7.0: the running build, all 89 `public/` files, all 263 `db/` files and every server script in use were identical; the network boundary was in place (49.7.3). Not in step: the live systemd unit was still the first baseline (`ProtectSystem=full`) - the Stage 49.7 sandbox had never been installed, and as written it would have blocked `/opt/erp/audit-archives`. Fixed that path, installed it with the new `deploy/install_service_unit.sh` (keeps the old unit, rolls back by itself if the ERP is not healthy in 30 s, then proves the sandbox from inside the running service): exposure 8.4 → 3.6. From now on `deploy.ps1` ships the unit and its installer, `remote_deploy.sh` installs it whenever it differs, and the Deploy click (also `-Check`, even when the build is already live) reports whether the live unit matches the repo. Still open: PostgreSQL least-privilege roles (`postgres_harden.sql`, 49.7.1/49.7.4) were most likely never run on prod - it needs the ERP to connect as `erp_app`, a planned change of its own. *Status: Done*

Methods: the three the user named (single multiplier; PP bands each with a multiplier; weight + PP). Built: new master **MRPRule** (`migrations_stage114_6_mrp_rules.sql`, applied by hand to 5490; Setup → MRP Rule, codes MRP00001 - "MR" is Material Receipt's) with method, multiplier, bands text (`500=3, 2000=2.5, above=2`), rate per gram, Default tick; `ProductType.mrp_rule`; `engines/mrp_rules.go` - the rule works on the GRN price incl. Built: `prefix_configs.year_position` ('' before the number, END at the very end, after any suffix; new `migrations_stage115_1_series_year_position.sql`, applied by hand to the 5490 DB - **GenerateSequence reads the column, so a database must be migrated before this build runs**); one layout function `joinNumberParts` shared by issuing and the save checks; "Where the year goes" on the Number Series form, preview follows; the handler keeps the stored value when a caller omits it. *Verified 2026-10-19:* `TestNumberLayoutYearAtEnd`, `TestNumberSeriesDeletedStoreAndYearAtEndDB` (a provisioned tenant issues ASNHO00001+2627) and every numbering test pass; browser - set At the end → preview ASN000012627, saved, reopened as At the end (dev row restored after). *Status: Done*
- [x] **115.2 Saving a series fails on a store with no usable code** (user, 2026-10-19: *"issue in saving"* - error: the number at store `Location/HQ/2026/000001` would be `SMLocation/HQ/2026/00000126270001`, not a valid GST number, "give that store a Code in number"; the store table the user sees shows HO). A store whose record id is a document-style number has no Short Code, so its id is used in the number. Cause: the form lists live stores only (its own check passed: "16 of 16"), but the server checked every store's sample number including **deleted** ones - a deleted store the form cannot show. Fix: deleted stores keep their code reserved (the two-stores-one-code check) but their sample is no longer held against the GST rules; the message names the store. *Verified:* `TestSeriesSaveIgnoresDeletedStoreSample` (deleted junk store passes; the same store live is refused, by name; its code stays taken) and the DB test above. Not checked against prod's records (no prod access) - if the store is live, the form now names it. *Status: Done*

## Stage 116 — Frozen table headers: rows showed above them on every scroll (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's Prefix Configurations screenshot)

| Phase | Status |
|---|---|
| 116.1 | Done |

- [x] **116.1 Rows scroll through a band above the frozen header** (user, 2026-10-19: *"ui is breaking fix this every scroll of page"*). Cause, measured on a scratch server: `.page-container` (the page scroller) had 24px top padding, and a sticky header sticks at the padding edge, so after scrolling the header sat 24px below the scroller's top with table cells painted in that band. Fix: the top gap is a `::before` spacer inside the scroll (`--view-pad-top` / `--view-gap`; the RF shell and transaction-screen overrides set them), so the page looks the same at rest and headers freeze flush at the top. *Verified 2026-10-19:* scratch server, Prefix Configurations scrolled 600px - before: header 24px below the scroller top, a TD painted in the band; after: header flush (gap 0), the band is the TH; at rest the page still starts 24px down; transaction screen keeps its 10px top at 1400 and 390px wide. *Status: Done*

The silent path (QZ Tray + a TSPL Printer record) was not set up on that PC.

| Phase | Status |
|---|---|
| 119.1 | Built, not verified |

- [ ] **119.1 Four Seznik built-ins: horizontal × 1, horizontal × 3, vertical × 1, vertical × 3.** Named by how the tag lies; three lying tags stack down the roll (100 mm tags cannot sit side by side on a 4-inch printer), three standing tags sit side by side. Replaces 113.4's single 3-up design. Built: `READY_DESIGNS` jd-seznik-h1 (keeps the shipped code `SEZNIK-TAG-100X14-3`, so a saved copy still counts as it), -h3 (`per_col: 3`), -v1 / -v3 (`direction: 'vertical'`, v3 3 across, 2 mm gap) - one design, the settings differ. *Verified (Chromium, real modules):* TSPL `SIZE` 100×14.3 / 100×48.9 / 14.3×100 / 46.9×100 mm; bitmaps and gallery cards right. Not printed on a roll. *Status: Built, not verified*
- [ ] **119.2 Print direction on every template** - Horizontal (as designed) or Vertical (turned a quarter), a template setting, so any design prints either way without redrawing it. Built: `settings.direction`; engine `cellSize` / `cellOrigin` / `drawCell` used by the printer bitmap, TSPL/ZPL, browser print (roll and A4), `paintPreview({asPrinted})` and new `paintPrint` (cards show one whole print now - Square shows its 2-up row). Studio: Direction on the Label tab's Roll section; a vertical design is edited as it reads (no ghosts), the new **One print** view shows it turned. *Verified:* studio Label tab driven in Chromium, no errors. *Status: Built, not verified*
- [ ] **119.3 Labels down** - how many labels one print stacks, with the ↕ gap between them, next to Across. Built: `settings.per_col` (1-10), print height = down × cell + gaps, `segmentRows` packs across × down; studio Down field, ghosts in a grid, rulers/zoom follow. *Status: Built, not verified*
- [ ] **119.4 A clean browser print** - the page the browser sends is the label's own size and the image is whole printer dots, so the dialog no longer lands the tag on the driver's paper or smears the barcode. Built: roll PNGs 1-bit at the Printer record's DPI (else the template's), `image-rendering: pixelated`; the first page size is also the default `@page`. *Verified:* Chromium PDF of a print is 46.9 × 100 mm (v3) / 100 × 48.9 mm (h3), one page. The Windows driver can still override the paper - the guide says so; the real fix is the silent path. *Status: Built, not verified*
- [ ] **119.5 Remember the template for a product type from the Print screen** - change it there and keep it as the Product type rule (Template rules stays the other place to change it). Built: preview lines carry `product_type`; **Keep for *(type)*** beside a picked template (per line, and for every line) writes `ProductType.sticker_template` through the rules tab's `patchDoc` (`rememberProductTypeTemplate`); lines then show "(product type)". *Verified:* engines sticker tests on the 5490 DB incl. a new product_type check; the real view-printing.js driven in Chromium against a scripted API (POST body, line text, toast). *Status: Built, not verified*
- [ ] **119.6 Guide: silent printing and where each print setting lives** - QZ Tray + Printer record (TSPL, DPI), direction, across, down, gaps. USER_GUIDE: four Seznik designs, Roll (Direction, Down, One print), Keep for, and Where the labels go (PrintBridge must be built for the exact ERP address; Printer record fields; driver paper size). Also: this PC's PrintBridge trusted only `http://localhost:8080`, so app.wholeops.in never reached it - `printbridge-setup-app.wholeops.in.exe` built to the Desktop for the user to run. *Status: Done*

- [ ] **120.6 Client branding in a SaaS** (user, 2026-10-19: *"where user can type his/her brand name and it will poulate as well in ERP in top placeholder is it good or wrong. suggest"*, *"or upload their logo. change erp theme as per their brand color"*, *"Is the brand logo upload happen in in their custom erp?"*, *"so if make saas i should not allow"*). Today a client's logo exists only inside Print Template Studio / Sticker Studio designs (PNG/JPG, 400 KB) - on documents and labels, not in the app shell. Recommended (spec §3, §6): allow, controlled - company name set once by the org admin and shown as company context in the header and tab title; company logo on documents (exists) and optionally a small company chip in the header; full white-label (their logo top-left, "Powered by Wholeops" at the bottom, their accent colour with an automatic contrast check, their logo on their own subdomain's sign-in page) as a per-tenant branding mode the plan unlocks - never per-user free text, never replacing the Wholeops product brand on the standard plan; raster uploads only (no SVG). A mockup (teal accent, placeholder client logo, "Powered by Wholeops") was shown on screenshots, no code. User, 2026-10-19: *"theme later we will build."* - parked. *Status: Needs decision*

## Stage 122 — Configuration → Item fields: the column header freezes while scrolling (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's Item fields screenshot)

| Phase | Status |
|---|---|
| 122.1 | Done |

- [x] **122.1 Item fields header scrolls away** (user, 2026-10-19: *"If i scroll in config, I will not no what is the config on/ off for. freeze it. and it sould not go up than the header"*). Cause: three scroll boxes between the table and the page scroller (the Configuration panel's `overflow:hidden`, `#config-fields`' `overflow:auto`, the table's own `overflow-x:auto` wrapper) plus `border-collapse: collapse` on `.item-fields-table`, so the global sticky `th` (Stage 41/116) stuck to a box that never scrolls. Fix (public/view-admin.js, view-item-form.js): drop the three overflows (the rail rounds its own corners), separate borders. Every other table on Configuration freezes the same way now. Below 820px the app-wide rule (styles.css: a `.table-panel` scrolls sideways, headers not frozen) still applies, unchanged. *Verified 2026-10-19:* scratch server, Item fields scrolled ~1000px - header top 99 = top-bar bottom and painted over the rows at 1440 and 1100 wide. *Status: Done*

## Stage 123 — Number Series form: the GRN batch series shows year boxes; "No year" will not select (opened 2026-10-19 from the user's Number Series - GRN batch number + VE screenshots)

| Phase | Status |
|---|---|
| 123.1 | Done |
| 123.2 | Done |
| 123.3 | Needs decision |

- [x] **123.1 GRN batch number shows "Year in the number"** (user, 2026-10-19: *"What is this bug"* - GRN batch number, Every day, Year 2627, At the end, preview GRN0001261019). A batch number prints its receipt date, so the year format does nothing; the form set `hidden` on both year boxes (Stages 86.2, 115.1) but `.form-group { display: flex }` overrides `hidden`, so neither box ever hid. Fix: `.form-group[hidden]` hides. "Where the year goes" stays visible for this series as "Where the date goes", because the server applies it to the date (`joinNumberParts`) and hiding it would leave a saved "At the end" in force out of sight. *Verified 2026-10-19 (browser, dev DB):* GRN batch shows no year box, "Where the date goes", preview B-261019-0001; cold core 184,244 / 184,320. *Status: Done*
- [x] **123.2 "No year" cannot be picked** (user, 2026-10-19: *"No year not selecting"* - VE series, Every financial year). "No year" was only the placeholder the year box shows when Restart numbering is Never; `previewEditPrefix` put the previous format back whenever the box was blank. A yearly restart with no year in the number would repeat numbers every April, so picking "No year" now sets Restart numbering to "Never - no year in the number". *Verified 2026-10-19 (browser):* ASN series - No year → Never, preview ASN/B59-LOC/00001; back to yearly restores 26-27; 2627 picks normally. Nothing saved. *Status: Done*
- [ ] **123.3 Type a store's own code** (user, 2026-10-19, prod Setup → Location: *"I want to edit user's code of store"* - BLR Warehouse is still Location/HQ/2026/000001; the Renumber panel only offers a prefix + running digits). The renumber engine (`engines/master_recode.go` `recodeInSchemaTx`) already rewrites every reference from an old → new list, so typed codes are possible. [needs design decision: only while a store is on an old long code, or any time from its Edit form; stores only or every master] *Status: Needs decision*
